output.md
The log field table, draft, parsing, checks and derivation are implemented and the 11 tests pass, but the commit is not made. The checkout has no git identity configured, and I didn't set one. The five files are staged; run `git commit` with your identity to finish.

**Changed**
- `CardioLogField.kt` (new): the 12-value `CardioLogField` enum.
- `CardioActivity.kt`: `logFields` in sheet order for all six activities.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt` (new): the immutable draft with `text`, `with`, `left`, `shownError`, `errors`, `canSubmit`, `derived`, `toRequest`, `fresh` and `of`. Range checks use the backend's ranges and the `between X and Y unit` messages. The rower's distance is sent in metres.
- `CardioFormat.kt`: `fixed` changed from private to internal so the draft can reuse it.

**Tests**
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` (new, in `commonTest`): 11 tests, one per named family.
- RED: the run failed to compile against the missing API.
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat`: ran; changed only my files.
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests 'CardioLogDraftTest'`: BUILD SUCCESSFUL, 11/11 passed.

**Deviations from the spec**
- The spec says the header holds the fourteen names, but it lists 11. I wrote the 11.
- The spec's `CT/` and `BC/` paths don't exist in this checkout. I put the test in `commonTest`, and I could not check `logFields` against the backend's `CardioActivity.kt`. I used the spec's table, which matches the design notes.
- ktlint requires a file named after its class, so the enum is in `CardioLogField.kt`, not `CardioLogFields.kt`.
- Choices the spec left open: pace and speed derivation as specified; elevation range is 0–65617 ft for mile workouts (0–20000 m otherwise); a typed non-number in Effort clears the field; non-numeric entries show "use a number" or "use a whole number"; the L4 rower figures were not available, so the test uses 2000 m in 8:24, which gives 2:06.

+private const val CADENCE_MAX = 250.0
+private const val ROWER_CADENCE_MIN = 10.0
+private const val ROWER_CADENCE_MAX = 60.0
+private const val POWER_MAX = 2_500.0
+private const val CALORIES_MAX = 20_000.0
+
+private val HOURS_MINUTES_SECONDS = Regex("""(\d{1,3}):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d)""")
+private val MINUTES_SECONDS = Regex("""(\d{1,3}):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+/**
+ * A cardio log being typed: the text of each field, the effort and note, and the fields the user has
+ * left. Pure — no Compose; the sheet debounces [errors] and calls [toRequest] on submit.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        activity.logFields
+            .filter { typed(it).isNotEmpty() }
+            .mapNotNull { field -> problem(field)?.let { field to it } }
+            .toMap()
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parseDuration(typed(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (typed(field).isNotEmpty()) return null
+        val seconds = parseDuration(typed(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        val distance = distance()
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> CardioFormat.derivedPace(seconds, distance, null, activity, units)?.let { clock(it) }
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(seconds, distance, null, units)?.let { fixed(it, 1) }
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = requireNotNull(parseDuration(typed(CardioLogField.DURATION))),
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parseMinutesSeconds(typed(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            maxHeartRate = typed(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            calories = typed(CardioLogField.CALORIES).toIntOrNull(),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = typed(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgCadence = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgPower = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER).toIntOrNull(),
+        )
+
+    private fun typed(field: CardioLogField): String = text(field).trim()
+
+    private fun distanceUnit(): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? = typed(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit()) }
+
+    private fun metricUnit(metric: CardioMetric): String = metric.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty()
+
+    private fun problem(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val text = typed(field)
+        val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+        val imperial = units == DistanceUnit.MI
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+                checked(text, ::parseDuration, "use h:mm:ss") { seconds ->
+                    between(seconds.toDouble(), 1.0, DURATION_MAX) { durationText(it.toInt()) }
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+                checked(text, { it.toDoubleOrNull() }, "use a number") { value ->
+                    if (rower) {
+                        between(value, 1.0, ROWER_DISTANCE_MAX, "m")
+                    } else {
+                        between(
+                            value,
+                            DISTANCE_MIN_KM,
+                            if (imperial) DISTANCE_MAX_MI else DISTANCE_MAX_KM,
+                            distanceUnit().name.lowercase(),
+                        ) {
+                            fixed(it, 2)
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                checked(text, ::parseMinutesSeconds, "use m:ss") { seconds ->
+                    between(seconds.toDouble(), PACE_MIN, if (rower) ROWER_PACE_MAX else PACE_MAX, metricUnit(CardioMetric.PACE)) {
+                        clock(it.toInt())
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                checked(text, { it.toDoubleOrNull() }, "use a number") { value ->
+                    between(value, SPEED_MIN, SPEED_MAX, metricUnit(CardioMetric.SPEED)) { fixed(it, 1) }
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+                if (imperial) {
+                    whole(text, 0.0, ELEVATION_MAX_FT, "ft")
+                } else {
+                    whole(text, 0.0, ELEVATION_MAX_M, "m")
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE -> {
+                whole(text, HEART_RATE_MIN, HEART_RATE_MAX, "bpm")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                whole(text, HEART_RATE_MIN, HEART_RATE_MAX, "bpm") ?: maxBelowAverage()
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+                if (rower) {
+                    whole(text, ROWER_CADENCE_MIN, ROWER_CADENCE_MAX, metricUnit(CardioMetric.CADENCE))
+                } else {
+                    whole(text, 1.0, CADENCE_MAX, metricUnit(CardioMetric.CADENCE))
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+                whole(text, 1.0, POWER_MAX, metricUnit(CardioMetric.POWER))
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+                whole(text, 0.0, CALORIES_MAX, "kcal")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun maxBelowAverage(): String? {
+        val avg = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        val max = typed(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        return if (max < avg) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val startedAt = workout.startedAt
+            val elapsed =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && startedAt != null) {
+                    (now - startedAt).inWholeSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0L).toInt()
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(it)) }.orEmpty(),
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = requireNotNull(workout.log)
+            val texts =
+                buildMap<CardioLogField, String> {
+                    fun add(
+                        field: CardioLogField,
+                        value: Any?,
+                    ) {
+                        if (value != null) put(field, value.toString())
+                    }
+
+                    add(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationText(log.durationSeconds))
+                    add(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, log.distance?.let { distanceText(it, workout) })
+                    add(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { clock(it) })
+                    add(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, log.avgSpeed?.let { number(it) })
+                    add(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, log.elevationGain)
+                    add(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, log.avgHeartRate)
+                    add(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, log.maxHeartRate)
+                    add(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, log.avgCadence)
+                    add(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, log.avgPower)
+                    add(CardioLogField.CALORIES, log.calories)
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                effort = log.effort,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? =
+    HOURS_MINUTES_SECONDS.matchEntire(text)?.let {
+        val (h, m, s) = it.destructured
+        h.toInt() * HOUR + m.toInt() * 60 + s.toInt()
+    } ?: parseMinutesSeconds(text)
+
+private fun parseMinutesSeconds(text: String): Int? =
+    MINUTES_SECONDS.matchEntire(text)?.let {
+        val (m, s) = it.destructured
+        m.toInt() * 60 + s.toInt()
+    }
+
+private fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private fun distanceText(
+    distance: CardioDistance,
+    workout: CardioWorkout,
+): String {
+    val basis = if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 1.0 else workout.distanceUnit.metres
+    val value = distance.value * distance.unit.metres / basis
+    return number((value * 1000).roundToLong() / 1000.0)
+}
+
+private fun <T : Any> checked(
+    text: String,
+    parse: (String) -> T?,
+    shape: String,
+    check: (T) -> String?,
+): String? {
+    val value = parse(text) ?: return shape
+    return check(value)
+}
+
+private fun whole(
+    text: String,
+    min: Double,
+    max: Double,
+    unit: String,
+): String? = checked(text, { it.toIntOrNull() }, "use a whole number") { between(it.toDouble(), min, max, unit) }
+
+private fun between(
+    value: Double,
+    min: Double,
+    max: Double,
+    unit: String = "",
+    show: (Double) -> String = { number(it) },
+): String? {
+    if (value in min..max) return null
+    val range = "between ${show(min)} and ${show(max)}"
+    return if (unit.isEmpty()) range else "$range $unit"
+}

+    DURATION,

+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,

+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Intervals",
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+        notes = notes,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        vararg typed: Pair<CardioLogField, String>,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        typed.fold(CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity, unit), now)) { draft, (field, value) ->
+            draft.with(field, value)
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft(typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft(typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { text ->
+            val errors = draft(typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to text)).errors()
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", errors[CardioLogField.DURATION], text)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val run = draft(typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12", CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02"))
+        assertEquals("6:37", run.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(run.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val ride =
+            draft(
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE,
+                typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40", CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "38.6"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("31.9", ride.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val rower =
+            draft(
+                CardioActivity.ROWER,
+                typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to "8:24", CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "2000"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("2:06", rower.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val run =
+            draft(
+                typed =
+                    arrayOf(
+                        CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "139",
+                    ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", run.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(run.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val rower = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to "8"))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", rower.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val run = draft(typed = arrayOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "20"))
+        val message = run.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE]
+        assertEquals("between 30 and 250 bpm", message)
+        assertNull(run.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals(message, run.left(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00Z"))
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(started, now).text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                maxHeartRate = 171,
+                calories = 540,
+                effort = 7,
+                elevationGain = 40,
+                avgCadence = 168,
+            )
+        val saved = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", saved.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", saved.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", saved.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", saved.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("150", saved.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("171", saved.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("540", saved.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("40", saved.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("168", saved.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("7", saved.text(CardioLogField.EFFORT))
+        assertEquals(7, saved.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", saved.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val run =
+            draft(
+                typed =
+                    arrayOf(
+                        CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                        CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+                        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "150",
+                    ),
+            ).copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                effort = 6,
+            ),
+            run.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}

The log draft logic is in place and the target tests pass, but I did not commit. Git has no author identity configured here, and I didn't set one. The four files are staged; commit them once an identity is set.

**Changed**
- `shared/.../cardio/CardioLogField.kt` (new): the `CardioLogField` enum. The spec named it `CardioLogFields.kt`, but ktlint requires the file to match the class name.
- `shared/.../cardio/CardioActivity.kt`: `logFields` per activity, in sheet order, per the spec's table.
- `shared/.../cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt` (new): immutable data class with `text`, `with`, `left`, `shownError`, `errors`, `canSubmit`, `derived`, `toRequest`, and the `fresh` / `of` companions. Duration parsing, the backend range messages, and derived pace/speed are all here.
- `shared/src/commonTest/.../cardio/CardioLogDraftTest.kt` (new): 11 tests.

**Commands run**

- `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests 'com.gymie.shared.cardio.*'`: 78 tests, 0 failures.
- `git commit`: failed with "Author identity unknown".

**Things to check**
- The backend `BC/core/domain/cardio/CardioActivity.kt` and `CardioLogSpecification.kt` are not in this environment. I could not verify the field table or ranges against them; I used the spec's tables.
- The spec says "fourteen names" but lists eleven. I wrote the eleven listed.
- The spec's "148 / 139" for max-below-average: I read it as avg 148, max 139.
- Choices the spec didn't cover: "use a number" for non-numeric numeric input, "use m:ss" for a bad pace, and range messages for duration (`between 1 and 86400 s`) and distance (`between 0 and 1000 km`, where the miles case is shown as `621.37 mi`). Effort has no range check.
- Distance is stored as typed in the workout's unit; the rower's is in metres.

To finish, set an identity and run the commit, or tell me which author to use and I'll commit with it.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val HOUR_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20000
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE_BPM = 250
+private const val MAX_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+
+/**
+ * A cardio log being typed: every field as text, the effort and note as values, and the fields the
+ * user has left. Immutable; the sheet holds one and replaces it on each edit.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val distanceUnit: DistanceUnit,
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        CardioLogField.entries.mapNotNull { field -> fieldError(field)?.let { field to it } }.toMap()
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parseDuration(text(DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        val duration = parseDuration(text(DURATION))
+        val distance = distance()
+        return when (field) {
+            AVG_PACE -> CardioFormat.derivedPace(duration, distance, null, activity, distanceUnit)?.let(::clock)
+            AVG_SPEED -> CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(duration, distance, null, distanceUnit)?.let(::shown)
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = requireNotNull(parseDuration(text(DURATION))) { "a log needs a duration" },
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parseClock(text(AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = text(AVG_SPEED).toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = text(AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            maxHeartRate = text(MAX_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            calories = text(CALORIES).toIntOrNull(),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = text(ELEVATION_GAIN).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgCadence = text(AVG_CADENCE).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgPower = text(AVG_POWER).toIntOrNull(),
+        )
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val value = text(DISTANCE).toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, distanceEntryUnit())
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceEntryUnit(): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else distanceUnit
+
+    private fun label(metric: CardioMetric): String = metric.unitLabel(activity, distanceUnit).orEmpty()
+
+    private fun fieldError(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val typed = text(field)
+        if (typed.isBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            DURATION -> durationError(typed)
+            DISTANCE -> distanceError(typed)
+            AVG_PACE -> paceError(typed)
+            AVG_SPEED -> numberError(typed, 0.1, 120.0, label(CardioMetric.SPEED))
+            ELEVATION_GAIN -> intError(typed, 0, MAX_ELEVATION, if (distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m")
+            AVG_HEART_RATE -> intError(typed, MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE_BPM, "bpm")
+            MAX_HEART_RATE -> intError(typed, MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE_BPM, "bpm") ?: maxBelowAverage(typed)
+            AVG_CADENCE -> cadenceError(typed)
+            AVG_POWER -> intError(typed, 1, MAX_POWER, "W")
+            CALORIES -> intError(typed, 0, MAX_CALORIES, "kcal")
+            EFFORT, NOTE -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun durationError(typed: String): String? {
+        val seconds = parseDuration(typed) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+        return if (seconds in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) null else between(1.0, MAX_DURATION_SECONDS.toDouble(), "s")
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceError(typed: String): String? {
+        val value = typed.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return "use a number"
+        val unit = distanceEntryUnit()
+        return if (value > 0 && value * unit.metres < MAX_METRES) {
+            null
+        } else {
+            between(0.0, MAX_METRES / unit.metres, unit.name.lowercase())
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun paceError(typed: String): String? {
+        val seconds = parseClock(typed) ?: return "use m:ss"
+        val (min, max) = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 60 to 600 else 60 to 3600
+        return if (seconds in min..max) null else "between ${clock(min)} and ${clock(max)} ${label(CardioMetric.PACE)}"
+    }
+
+    private fun cadenceError(typed: String): String? =
+        if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+            intError(typed, 10, 60, "spm")
+        } else {
+            intError(typed, 1, 250, label(CardioMetric.CADENCE))
+        }
+
+    private fun maxBelowAverage(typed: String): String? {
+        val average = text(AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        val max = typed.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        return if (max < average) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun intError(
+        typed: String,
+        min: Int,
+        max: Int,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val value = typed.toIntOrNull() ?: return "use a number"
+        return if (value in min..max) null else between(min.toDouble(), max.toDouble(), unit)
+    }
+
+    private fun numberError(
+        typed: String,
+        min: Double,
+        max: Double,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val value = typed.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return "use a number"
+        return if (value in min..max) null else between(min, max, unit)
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val elapsed =
+                workout.startedAt
+                    ?.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+                    ?.let { (now - it).inWholeSeconds.toInt().coerceAtLeast(0) }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                distanceUnit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(DURATION to formatDuration(it)) }.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = requireNotNull(workout.log) { "a saved log is needed to edit one" }
+            val draft = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, log.effort, workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val texts =
+                buildMap {
+                    put(DURATION, formatDuration(log.durationSeconds))
+                    log.distance?.let { put(DISTANCE, shown(it.value * it.unit.metres / draft.distanceEntryUnit().metres)) }
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(AVG_PACE, clock(it)) }
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { put(AVG_SPEED, shown(it)) }
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { put(ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { put(AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { put(MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { put(AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgPower?.let { put(AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+                    log.calories?.let { put(CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+                }
+            return draft.copy(texts = texts)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun between(
+    min: Double,
+    max: Double,
+    unit: String,
+) = "between ${shown(min)} and ${shown(max)} $unit"
+
+private fun shown(value: Double): String = number((value * 100).roundToLong() / 100.0)
+
+private fun formatDuration(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR_SECONDS) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.split(":")
+    return when (parts.size) {
+        2 -> parseClock(text)
+        3 -> parseHourClock(parts)
+        else -> null
+    }
+}
+
+private fun parseHourClock(parts: List<String>): Int? {
+    val hours = wholeNumber(parts[0]) ?: return null
+    val minutes = twoDigits(parts[1]) ?: return null
+    val seconds = twoDigits(parts[2]) ?: return null
+    return hours * HOUR_SECONDS + minutes * 60 + seconds
+}
+
+private fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.split(":")
+    if (parts.size != 2) return null
+    return wholeNumber(parts[0])?.let { minutes -> twoDigits(parts[1])?.let { seconds -> minutes * 60 + seconds } }
+}
+
+private fun wholeNumber(part: String): Int? = part.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && it.all(Char::isDigit) }?.toIntOrNull()
+
+private fun twoDigits(part: String): Int? = part.takeIf { it.length == 2 }?.let(::wholeNumber)?.takeIf { it < 60 }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val km = DistanceUnit.KM
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = km,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Session",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draftOf(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = km,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity, unit), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z"))
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        val draft = draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+
+        assertEquals(1992, draft.with(DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft.with(DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        val draft = draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { typed ->
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.with(DURATION, typed).errors()[DURATION], typed)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE)
+                .with(DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "38.6")
+
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(DURATION, "8:24")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "2000")
+
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", draft.errors()[MAX_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(DURATION, "8:24")
+                .with(AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val draft =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(AVG_CADENCE, "300")
+
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 250 spm", draft.errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertNull(draft.shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 250 spm", draft.left(AVG_CADENCE).shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started =
+            workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00Z"))
+
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(started, Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z"))
+
+        assertEquals("41:06", draft.text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val saved =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, km),
+                avgHeartRate = 148,
+                maxHeartRate = 171,
+                avgCadence = 170,
+                elevationGain = 40,
+                calories = 512,
+                effort = 6,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED, log = saved, notes = "hilly"))
+
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", draft.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("148", draft.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("171", draft.text(MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("170", draft.text(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(6, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val run =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "150")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+
+        val request = run.toRequest()
+
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, km), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(150, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+
+        val rower =
+            draftOf(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(DURATION, "8:24")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "7120")
+
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), rower.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+}

output.md
The log field table, draft and tests are in place and green, but I did not commit. Git has no author identity configured here, and I didn't set one. The five files are staged on `master`; run `git commit` once `user.name` and `user.email` are set, using the message in the task's commit step plus the `Co-Authored-By` line.

**What changed**
- `CardioLogField.kt`: the `CardioLogField` enum. It lives in its own file because ktlint requires a single-class file to be named after the class.
- `CardioLogFields.kt`: `cardioLogFields(activity)`, the per-activity field table in sheet order.
- `CardioActivity.kt`: a `logFields` member that delegates to that table.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt`: the immutable draft. It covers text per field, `effort`, `note`, `left`, `errors()` against the backend ranges, `derived()` via `CardioFormat.derivedPace`/`derivedSpeed`, `canSubmit`, `toRequest()`, and the `fresh`/`of` factories.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt`: 12 tests.

**Tests**
- 11 of the spec's named tests, plus `theRowerLogsDistanceInMetres`. The spec said "fourteen names" but listed eleven, so I added the one it requires separately.
- RED: `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` failed to compile (`Unresolved reference 'CardioLogDraft'`).
- GREEN: the 12 tests pass.
- Ran `:shared:ktlintFormat`, then `:shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests 'CardioLogDraftTest'`. All pass.
- The whole `com.gymie.shared.cardio.*` package also passes: 80 tests across 9 suites.
- `--offline` fails on missing cached dependencies, so I ran the Gradle commands online.

**Gaps and judgement calls**
- The backend `CardioActivity.kt` is not on this machine, so I could not check the field table against it. The table follows the spec and the design decision.
- The rower example uses 29:54 over 7120 m. I picked it because it yields the stated 2:06; the frame's actual figures were not available.
- The spec gives no exact message text for pace, speed and distance ranges. I formatted them as `between 1:00 and 60:00 /km`, `between 0.1 and 120.0 km/h` and `between 0.01 and 1000 km`.
- Work is on `master`, not the `spectre/<name>` branch the design doc asks for, because the task didn't say to branch.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val SECONDS_PER_MINUTE = 60
+private const val SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE = 250
+private const val MAX_CADENCE = 250
+private const val MIN_ROWER_CADENCE = 10
+private const val MAX_ROWER_CADENCE = 60
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20000
+private const val CENTS = 100.0
+private const val TENTHS = 10.0
+
+private val CLOCK = Regex("""(\d+):([0-5]\d)""")
+private val HOUR_CLOCK = Regex("""(\d+):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+/**
+ * One cardio log being typed: each field's text, the effort and note, and the fields the user has left.
+ * Pure: the sheet owns the 400 ms wait before errors show, and the request carries typed values only.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        buildMap {
+            CardioLogField.entries.forEach { field -> problem(field)?.let { put(field, it) } }
+        }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = durationSeconds() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, activity, units)?.let(::clock)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, units)?.let(::oneDecimal)
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = requireNotNull(durationSeconds()),
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parseClock(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            maxHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            calories = text(CardioLogField.CALORIES).toIntOrNull(),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgCadence = text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgPower = text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER).toIntOrNull(),
+        )
+
+    private fun durationSeconds(): Int? = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+
+    private fun distanceUnit(): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit()) }
+
+    private fun problem(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val typed = text(field)
+        if (typed.isBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> durationProblem(typed)
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> distanceProblem(typed)
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> paceProblem(typed)
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> speedProblem(typed)
+            CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> wholeProblem(typed, MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, "bpm") ?: belowAverage(typed)
+            else -> wholeRange(field)?.let { (min, max, unit) -> wholeProblem(typed, min, max, unit) }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun wholeRange(field: CardioLogField): Triple<Int, Int, String>? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> Triple(0, MAX_ELEVATION, if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m")
+            CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE -> Triple(MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, "bpm")
+            CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> cadenceRange()
+            CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> Triple(1, MAX_POWER, "W")
+            CardioLogField.CALORIES -> Triple(0, MAX_CALORIES, "kcal")
+            else -> null
+        }
+
+    private fun cadenceRange(): Triple<Int, Int, String> {
+        val unit = CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty()
+        return if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+            Triple(MIN_ROWER_CADENCE, MAX_ROWER_CADENCE, unit)
+        } else {
+            Triple(1, MAX_CADENCE, unit)
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun durationProblem(typed: String): String? {
+        val seconds = parseDuration(typed) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+        return if (seconds in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) null else between("1", MAX_DURATION_SECONDS.toString(), "s")
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceProblem(typed: String): String? {
+        val metres = typed.toDoubleOrNull()?.times(distanceUnit().metres)
+        if (metres != null && metres > 0 && metres < MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) return null
+        val upper = roundedTo(MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / distanceUnit().metres, CENTS)
+        return between("0.01", number(upper), distanceUnit().name.lowercase())
+    }
+
+    private fun paceProblem(typed: String): String? {
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+        val seconds = parseClock(typed)
+        if (seconds != null && seconds in MIN_PACE_SECONDS..max) return null
+        return between(clock(MIN_PACE_SECONDS), clock(max), CardioMetric.PACE.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty())
+    }
+
+    private fun speedProblem(typed: String): String? {
+        val speed = typed.toDoubleOrNull()
+        if (speed != null && speed in MIN_SPEED..MAX_SPEED) return null
+        return between("0.1", "120.0", CardioMetric.SPEED.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty())
+    }
+
+    private fun belowAverage(typed: String): String? {
+        val max = typed.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        val avg = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+        return if (max < avg) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun wholeProblem(
+        typed: String,
+        min: Int,
+        max: Int,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val value = typed.toIntOrNull()
+        return if (value != null && value in min..max) null else between(min.toString(), max.toString(), unit)
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val elapsed =
+                workout.startedAt
+                    ?.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+                    ?.let { (now - it).inWholeSeconds.toInt() }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(it)) }.orEmpty(),
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = log?.texts(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit).orEmpty(),
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun CardioLog.texts(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+    val distanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+    return buildMap {
+        put(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationText(durationSeconds))
+        distance?.let { put(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, number(inUnit(it, distanceUnit))) }
+        avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, clock(it)) }
+        avgSpeed?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, number(it)) }
+        elevationGain?.let { put(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+        avgHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+        maxHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+        avgCadence?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+        avgPower?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+        calories?.let { put(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun inUnit(
+    distance: CardioDistance,
+    unit: DistanceUnit,
+): Double =
+    if (distance.unit == unit) {
+        distance.value
+    } else {
+        roundedTo(distance.value * distance.unit.metres / unit.metres, CENTS)
+    }
+
+private fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < SECONDS_PER_HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val trimmed = text.trim()
+    HOUR_CLOCK.matchEntire(trimmed)?.let { match ->
+        val (hours, minutes, seconds) = match.destructured
+        return hours.toIntOrNull()?.let { it * SECONDS_PER_HOUR + minutes.toInt() * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + seconds.toInt() }
+    }
+    return parseClock(trimmed)
+}
+
+private fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val match = CLOCK.matchEntire(text.trim()) ?: return null
+    val (minutes, seconds) = match.destructured
+    return minutes.toIntOrNull()?.let { it * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + seconds.toInt() }
+}
+
+private fun between(
+    min: String,
+    max: String,
+    unit: String,
+) = "between $min and $max $unit"
+
+private fun roundedTo(
+    value: Double,
+    scale: Double,
+): Double = (value * scale).roundToLong() / scale
+
+private fun oneDecimal(value: Double): String = "${value.toLong()}.${(value * TENTHS).roundToLong() % 10}"

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+private val SESSION_TAIL = listOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES, CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE)
+private val HEART_RATE = listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+
+internal fun cardioLogFields(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) +
+                HEART_RATE + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE) + HEART_RATE + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+            ) + HEART_RATE + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED) +
+                HEART_RATE + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE) + HEART_RATE + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) +
+                HEART_RATE + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + SESSION_TAIL
+        }
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Session",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun fresh(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity, unit), now)
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        val run = fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+        assertEquals(1992, run.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, run.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        val run = fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { typed ->
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", run.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, typed).errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION], typed)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val run =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", run.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(run.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val ride =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", ride.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val row =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", row.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerLogsDistanceInMetres() {
+        val row =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), row.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val run =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", run.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val row = fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", row.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val run = fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "20")
+        val message = run.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE]
+        assertTrue(message != null)
+        assertNull(run.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals(message, run.left(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val inProgress =
+            workout(
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+                status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00Z"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(inProgress, now).text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val saved =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                maxHeartRate = 172,
+                calories = 620,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 80,
+                avgCadence = 170,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, log = saved, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("150", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("172", draft.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("620", draft.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("80", draft.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("170", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(6, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "140")
+                .with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, "400")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 140,
+                calories = 400,
+                effort = 6,
+            ),
+            draft.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}
